<body lang=FR style='tab-interval:35.4pt;word-wrap:break-word'>

<div class=WordSection1>

<div style='mso-element:para-border-div;border:solid windowtext 1.0pt;
mso-border-alt:solid windowtext .25pt;padding:0cm 0cm 0cm 0cm'>

<p class=MsoNormal style='mso-margin-top-alt:auto;mso-margin-bottom-alt:auto;
line-height:normal;mso-outline-level:1;border:none;mso-border-alt:solid windowtext .25pt;
padding:0cm;mso-padding-alt:0cm 0cm 0cm 0cm'><b><span style='font-size:24.0pt;
font-family:"pplxSerif",serif;mso-fareast-font-family:"Times New Roman";
mso-bidi-font-family:"Times New Roman";color:#27251E;mso-font-kerning:18.0pt;
mso-ligatures:none;mso-fareast-language:FR'>Onglet Exercices — Description
détaillée finale (Plan Comptable Tunisien)<o:p></o:p></span></b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='mso-margin-top-alt:auto;mso-margin-bottom-alt:auto;
line-height:normal;mso-outline-level:2;border:none;mso-border-alt:solid windowtext .25pt;
padding:0cm;mso-padding-alt:0cm 0cm 0cm 0cm'><b><span style='font-size:18.0pt;
font-family:"pplxSerif",serif;mso-fareast-font-family:"Times New Roman";
mso-bidi-font-family:"Times New Roman";color:#27251E;mso-font-kerning:0pt;
mso-ligatures:none;mso-fareast-language:FR'>1. Contexte et objectif<o:p></o:p></span></b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='mso-margin-top-alt:auto;mso-margin-bottom-alt:auto;
line-height:normal;border:none;mso-border-alt:solid windowtext .25pt;
padding:0cm;mso-padding-alt:0cm 0cm 0cm 0cm'><b><span style='font-family:"pplxSerif",serif;
mso-fareast-font-family:"Times New Roman";mso-bidi-font-family:"Times New Roman";
color:#27251E;border:solid windowtext 1.0pt;mso-border-alt:solid windowtext .25pt;
padding:0cm;mso-font-kerning:0pt;mso-ligatures:none;mso-fareast-language:FR'>Contexte</span></b><span
style='font-family:"pplxSerif",serif;mso-fareast-font-family:"Times New Roman";
mso-bidi-font-family:"Times New Roman";color:#27251E;mso-font-kerning:0pt;
mso-ligatures:none;mso-fareast-language:FR'>&nbsp;: Plan comptable tunisien,
avec comptes&nbsp;<b><span style='border:solid windowtext 1.0pt;mso-border-alt:
solid windowtext .25pt;padding:0cm'>131 &quot;Bénéfices&quot;</span></b>&nbsp;et&nbsp;<b><span
style='border:solid windowtext 1.0pt;mso-border-alt:solid windowtext .25pt;
padding:0cm'>135 &quot;Pertes&quot;</span></b>&nbsp;pour le résultat.<o:p></o:p></span></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='mso-margin-top-alt:auto;mso-margin-bottom-alt:auto;
line-height:normal;border:none;mso-border-alt:solid windowtext .25pt;
padding:0cm;mso-padding-alt:0cm 0cm 0cm 0cm'><b><span style='font-family:"pplxSerif",serif;
mso-fareast-font-family:"Times New Roman";mso-bidi-font-family:"Times New Roman";
color:#27251E;border:solid windowtext 1.0pt;mso-border-alt:solid windowtext .25pt;
padding:0cm;mso-font-kerning:0pt;mso-ligatures:none;mso-fareast-language:FR'>Objectif</span></b><span
style='font-family:"pplxSerif",serif;mso-fareast-font-family:"Times New Roman";
mso-bidi-font-family:"Times New Roman";color:#27251E;mso-font-kerning:0pt;
mso-ligatures:none;mso-fareast-language:FR'>&nbsp;: Gérer la création,
configuration, ouverture, clôture et&nbsp;<b><span style='border:solid windowtext 1.0pt;
mso-border-alt:solid windowtext .25pt;padding:0cm'>report à nouveau (RAN)</span></b>&nbsp;des
exercices comptables. Permettre le transfert automatique des soldes et
écritures non lettrées des&nbsp;<b><span style='border:solid windowtext 1.0pt;
mso-border-alt:solid windowtext .25pt;padding:0cm'>comptes de bilan (classes 1
à 5)</span></b>&nbsp;de l'exercice N vers N+1, avec&nbsp;<b><span
style='border:solid windowtext 1.0pt;mso-border-alt:solid windowtext .25pt;
padding:0cm'>deux modes de report</span></b>&nbsp;configurés&nbsp;<b><span
style='border:solid windowtext 1.0pt;mso-border-alt:solid windowtext .25pt;
padding:0cm'>au niveau de chaque compte</span></b>&nbsp;:&nbsp;<b><span
style='border:solid windowtext 1.0pt;mso-border-alt:solid windowtext .25pt;
padding:0cm'>Détail</span></b>&nbsp;(lignes non lettrées) et&nbsp;<b><span
style='border:solid windowtext 1.0pt;mso-border-alt:solid windowtext .25pt;
padding:0cm'>Solde</span></b>&nbsp;(solde cumulé), et équilibrage avec le
résultat via&nbsp;<b><span style='border:solid windowtext 1.0pt;mso-border-alt:
solid windowtext .25pt;padding:0cm'>131/135</span></b>.<o:p></o:p></span></p>

</div>

<hr size=1 width="100%" align=center>

</span></div>

<div style='mso-element:para-border-div;border:solid windowtext 1.0pt;

<p class=MsoNormal style='mso-margin-top-alt:auto;mso-margin-bottom-alt:auto;

<p class=MsoNormal style='mso-margin-top-alt:auto;mso-margin-bottom-alt:auto;
line-height:normal;mso-outline-level:2;border:none;mso-border-alt:solid windowtext .25pt;

Exercices<o:p></o:p></span></b></p>

</div>

<div style='mso-element:para-border-div;border:solid windowtext 1.0pt;

<ul type=disc>
 <li class=MsoNormal style='color:#27251E;mso-margin-top-alt:auto;mso-margin-bottom-alt:

     padding:0cm;mso-font-kerning:0pt;mso-ligatures:none;mso-fareast-language:
     FR'>Liste des exercices</span></b><span style='font-family:"pplxSerif",serif;
     mso-fareast-font-family:"Times New Roman";mso-bidi-font-family:"Times New Roman";
     mso-font-kerning:0pt;mso-ligatures:none;mso-fareast-language:FR'>&nbsp;(tableau)<o:p></o:p></span></li>

</div>

<div style='mso-element:para-border-div;border:solid windowtext 1.0pt;
mso-border-alt:solid windowtext .25pt;padding:0cm 0cm 0cm 0cm;margin-left:54.0pt;
margin-right:0cm'>

<ul type=disc>
 <ul type=disc>
  <li class=MsoNormal style='color:#27251E;mso-margin-top-alt:auto;mso-margin-bottom-alt:
      auto;margin-left:-18.0pt;line-height:normal;mso-list:l10 level2 lfo1;

      padding:0cm;mso-padding-alt:0cm 0cm 0cm 0cm'><span style='font-family:
      "pplxSerif",serif;mso-fareast-font-family:"Times New Roman";mso-bidi-font-family:
      "Times New Roman";mso-font-kerning:0pt;mso-ligatures:none;mso-fareast-language:
      FR'>Colonnes : Code exercice, Libellé, Date début, Date fin, État
      (Ouvert/Fermé/Clôturé), Lignes saisies, Actions<o:p></o:p></span></li>
  <li class=MsoNormal style='color:#27251E;mso-margin-top-alt:auto;mso-margin-bottom-alt:
      auto;margin-left:-18.0pt;line-height:normal;mso-list:l10 level2 lfo1;
      tab-stops:list 72.0pt;border:none;mso-border-alt:solid windowtext .25pt;
      padding:0cm;mso-padding-alt:0cm 0cm 0cm 0cm'><span style='font-family:
      "pplxSerif",serif;mso-fareast-font-family:"Times New Roman";mso-bidi-font-family:
      "Times New Roman";mso-font-kerning:0pt;mso-ligatures:none;mso-fareast-language:
      FR'>Actions par ligne :&nbsp;<b><span style='border:solid windowtext 1.0pt;
      mso-border-alt:solid windowtext .25pt;padding:0cm'>Voir</span></b>,&nbsp;<b><span
      style='border:solid windowtext 1.0pt;mso-border-alt:solid windowtext .25pt;
      padding:0cm'>Modifier</span></b>,&nbsp;<b><span style='border:solid windowtext 1.0pt;

      ouvert et vide)<o:p></o:p></span></li>
 </ul>

<ol start=1 type=1>
 <li class=MsoNormal style='color:#27251E;mso-margin-top-alt:auto;mso-margin-bottom-alt:

     FR'>Vérifier que&nbsp;<b><span style='border:solid windowtext 1.0pt;
     mso-border-alt:solid windowtext .25pt;padding:0cm'>tous les journaux sont
     validés</span></b>&nbsp;(pas de brouillon)<o:p></o:p></span></li>
 <li class=MsoNormal style='color:#27251E;mso-margin-top-alt:auto;mso-margin-bottom-alt:

     FR'>Vérifier que&nbsp;<span class=GramE><b><span style='border:solid windowtext 1.0pt;
     mso-border-alt:solid windowtext .25pt;padding:0cm'>SUM(</span></b></span><b><span
     style='border:solid windowtext 1.0pt;mso-border-alt:solid windowtext .25pt;
     padding:0cm'>débit) = <span class=GramE>SUM(</span>crédit)</span></b>&nbsp;sur
     l'exercice (balance équilibrée)<o:p></o:p></span></li>
 <li class=MsoNormal style='color:#27251E;mso-margin-top-alt:auto;mso-margin-bottom-alt:

     FR'>Vérifier qu'il&nbsp;<b><span style='border:solid windowtext 1.0pt;
